- **culture-onboarding-90**: Design a 90-day onboarding architecture for a role. Written-first elements for remote/hybrid (Buffer, GitLab patterns). Manager script + new-hire prep doc + retention-metric tie. The 90-day window predicts retention better than the entire interview process.
